<h5 class="et_pb_toggle_title">WE LOVE WHAT WE DO</h5>
<div class="et_pb_toggle_content clearfix">
<p><span data-mce-mark="1" style="box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; border: 0px; outline: 0px; background: 0px 0px; font-size: 18.6667px; vertical-align: baseline; text-size-adjust: 100%;">Yummy Sounds is a family run company and <span data-mce-mark="1" style="box-sizing: border-box; font-weight: bold; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; border: 0px; outline: 0px; background: 0px 0px; font-size: 18.6667px; vertical-align: baseline; text-size-adjust: 100%;">we love our baby!</span></span></p>
<p><span data-mce-mark="1" style="box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; border: 0px; outline: 0px; background: 0px 0px; font-size: 14pt; vertical-align: baseline; text-size-adjust: 100%;">We have developed Yummy Sounds since 2015 for <span class="mceItemHidden"><strong>commercial marketing agency’s, motion designers &amp; filmmaker</strong>.</span> I am motion designer &amp; composer myself and for my job I was looking for more music than I can create on my own. I spend hours &amp; days on stock music pages and <span class="mceItemHidden">I</span> got sick of listening through all the bad quality music.</span></p>
<p><span style="box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; border: 0px; outline: 0px; background: 0px 0px; font-size: 14pt; vertical-align: baseline; text-size-adjust: 100%;">I simply decided to watch out for <strong>the best composers in the world</strong> to create my own little library. So <span class="mceItemHidden">I</span> asked all the composers in the team to create a library with their <span class="mceItemHidden">world-class</span> music and they fully agreed, so <strong>we started Yummy Sounds together</strong> and <strong>we simply love it!</strong></span></p>
</div> <!-- .et_pb_toggle_content -->
</div> <!-- .et_pb_toggle --><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_toggle et_pb_toggle_3 et_pb_toggle_item et_pb_toggle_close">
<h5 class="et_pb_toggle_title">WORLD-CLASS &amp; HANDPICKED</h5>
<div class="et_pb_toggle_content clearfix">
<p><span style="font-size: 14pt;">Our composer team is small, but <strong>they are truly world-class.</strong> <strong>We select carefully our composers</strong> and listen to every single track to make sure we only have the best available music on Yummy Sounds. We know your time is valuable and you will notice on Yummy Sounds <strong>you find a lot faster what you are actually looking for.</strong></span></p>
<p><span style="font-size: 14pt;"><strong>Our composers are working really close with us</strong>, and they are always ready to make adjustments on all tracks from the library, and we also <strong>create custom and personalized sound designs</strong> for you.</span></p>
<p><span style="font-size: 14pt;">We are ready to handle this process for you <strong>fast, affordable and professional.</strong></span></p>
</div> <!-- .et_pb_toggle_content -->
</div> <!-- .et_pb_toggle --><div class="et_pb_module et_pb_toggle et_pb_toggle_4 et_pb_toggle_item et_pb_toggle_close">

<script>
	jQuery(function($){
		$('.ajax_add_to_cart').live('click',function(){
			$.ajax({
				type: "post",
				dataType: 'JSON',
				url: "https://www.yummy-sounds.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php",    
				async: false,
				data: {
					'action':'single_add_to_cart',
					'id': $(this).attr('id'),
				},
				context: this,
				success: function(data){
					$(this).addClass('removefromcart').removeClass('ajax_add_to_cart button et_pb_button').html('<i class="fa">×</i> &nbsp; Remove');
					var count = data['total_cart'];
					//Append Model Info
					var appendcard = '<div class="card animated slideInUp" id="addtocartcard"> <div class="div-row"><div class="col-xs-2" style="padding:0px"><img src="'+data['product_image']+'" class="media-object"/></div> <div class="col-xs-10" style="padding:0px"><h6 class="media-heading">'+data['product_name']+'</h6> <span><i class="icon-cart-2"></i> <a href="/cart">Go to Cart</a></span></div></div> </div>';
					$('.yummy-button.cart').append(appendcard);
					setTimeout(function() {
					  $('#addtocartcard').remove(); 
					}, 4000);
					$('.cart-count .header-cart-count').text(count);
					$('.view-cart').removeClass('hidden');
					
				},
				error: function(errorThrown){ 
					console.log('Error Found!');
				}
			});
		});
		
		//Remove From Cart
		$('.removefromcart').live('click',function(){
			
			$.ajax({
				type: "post",
				dataType: 'JSON',
				url: "https://www.yummy-sounds.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php",    
				async: false,
				data: {
					'action':'remove_from_cart',
					'id': $(this).attr('id'),
				},
				context: this,
				success: function(data){
					var count = data['totalcart'];
					$('.cart-count .header-cart-count').text(count);
					$(this).addClass('ajax_add_to_cart button et_pb_button').removeClass('removefromcart').html('<i class="icon-cart-2"></i> &nbsp; Add To Cart');
				},
				error: function(errorThrown){ 
					console.log('Error Found!');
				}
			});
		});	
	});
	</script>
<!--end of ajax add to cart-->

				$.ajax({
					type: "POST",

					data: {
						'action':'notification_cookies',
					},
					context: this,
					beforeSend: function(){
						$(this).closest('.bottom-notifications').slideUp();
					},
					success: function(response){
						console.log("Popop Removed");
					},
					error: function(errorThrown){ 
						console.log("Error Found");
					}
				});
			});
		});
	</script>

<script>
jQuery(function(){
	
// Set the date we're counting down to
var countDownDate = new Date("Aug 17, 2019 14:00:00").getTime();

// Update the count down every 1 second
var x = setInterval(function() {

    // Get todays date and time
    var now = new Date().getTime();
    
    // Find the distance between now an the count down date
    var distance = countDownDate - now;
    
    // Time calculations for days, hours, minutes and seconds
    var days = Math.floor(distance / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    var hours = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
    var minutes = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
    var seconds = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);
    
    // Output the result in an element with id="demo"
	
	jQuery('#countdown').html(days + "d " + hours + "h " + minutes + "m " + seconds + "s ");
	
    
    // If the count down is over, write some text 
    if (distance < 0) {
        clearInterval(x);
		jQuery('#countdown').html('EXPIRED');
    }
}, 1000);



});
</script>
